[746] m-leather_B — Macro close-up of leather grain and horsehair strands, filling the frame, macro photograph, shallow depth of field, soft light, warm light-grey tone, photorealistic, no text, full-bleed square frame with no white bars or borders, no people, no hands, no text, no logos, no watermark
[747] m-najeon_B — Macro close-up of iridescent mother-of-pearl pieces set in black lacquer, filling the frame, macro photograph, shallow depth of field, soft light, warm light-grey tone, photorealistic, no text, full-bleed square frame with no white bars or borders, no people, no hands, no text, no logos, no watermark
[748] m-onggi_B — Macro close-up of rough earthenware surface with natural glaze streaks, filling the frame, macro photograph, shallow depth of field, soft light, warm light-grey tone, photorealistic, no text, full-bleed square frame with no white bars or borders, no people, no hands, no text, no logos, no watermark
[749] m-paste_B — Macro close-up of a spoon of red fermented paste in an earthenware dish, filling the frame, macro photograph, shallow depth of field, soft light, warm light-grey tone, photorealistic, no text, full-bleed square frame with no white bars or borders, no people, no hands, no text, no logos, no watermark
[750] m-porcelain_B — Macro close-up of white glaze with brushed blue cobalt lines, filling the frame, macro photograph, shallow depth of field, soft light, warm light-grey tone, photorealistic, no text, full-bleed square frame with no white bars or borders, no people, no hands, no text, no logos, no watermark
